- Nov 13 12:25 UTC: PH agent on coverage above 100%: not double-counting. On 27 of 72 top-3 launches of Oct 1-24 the store alone holds more distinct upvoters (from profile upvote lists) than Product Hunt's displayed vote count (e.g. 679 vs 333, 411 vs 246, 505 vs 347), so the displayed count discounts or removes votes. Coverage figures are upvoters / displayed votes and overstate on top launches. Decisions: headline coverage capped at 1 per launch from now on, raw ratio reported separately; PH agent to check whether displayed counts fall after the day (removals) or were always below listed upvoters (discounting); E5 to model which voters' votes count (non-negative weights by voter traits) and recompute coverage as weighted votes.
- Nov 13 12:30 UTC: PH agent deciding check: the gap is discounting at count time, not removals. On 365 live-captured launches (Oct 31 - Nov 12) the displayed votesCount fell after day end on only 14 launches (17 votes of 39,481, 0.04%; none in the top 3, which rose by a median +45). Listed upvoters in the store exceed the day-end displayed count on 12 of 36 top-3 launches even with partial store coverage.
